\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]b:{C}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]c:{C}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma](\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:b,\iota_{r}\;{y}:c\}):{C}}\rrbracket

as desired.

•

If
e=()
is the null expression, since
\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket
is
pure, the desired result holds trivially since
\mathbf{1}
is the terminal object in the category
of pure morphisms.

We may now prove the soundness of substitution for regions as follows: assuming

\Gamma\vdash r\rhd\mathsf{L}
, we proceed by induction on
r
as follows:

•

If
r=\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;a
, then we have that

(222)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta\vdash\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;A\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta\vdash_{\bot}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\bot}[\gamma]a:{A}}\rrbracket;\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell}=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;a)\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

•

If
r=(\mathsf{let}\;x=a;t)
, then we have that, by induction,

(223)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{A}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{(\gamma,x\mapsto x):(\Gamma,x:A)\mapsto(\Delta,x:A)}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma,x\mapsto x]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

since
x
can be taken to be a free variable. Hence,

(224)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta\vdash\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\Delta_{\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Delta\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{A}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma](\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;t)\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

•

If
r=(\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;t)
, then we have that, by induction,

(225)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes(\llbracket{A}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\alpha;\llbracket{(\gamma,x\mapsto x,y\mapsto y):(\Gamma,x:A,y:B)\mapsto(\Delta,x:A,y:B)}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash[\gamma,x\mapsto x,y\mapsto y]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash[\gamma]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

since
x,y
can be taken to be free variables. Hence,

(226)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta\vdash\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;\;t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\Delta_{\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Delta\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes(\llbracket{A}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash[\gamma]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma](\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;\;t)\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

•

If
r=\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:s,\iota_{r}\;{y}:t\}
, then we have that, by induction

(227)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{A}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{(\gamma,x\mapsto x):(\Gamma,x:A)\mapsto(\Delta,x:A)}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma,x\mapsto x]s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma]s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

and

(228)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{(\gamma,y\mapsto y):(\Gamma,y:B)\mapsto(\Delta,y:B)}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash[\gamma,y\mapsto y]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash[\gamma]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

since
x,y
can be taken to be free variables. Hence,

(229)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket;\Delta_{\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Delta\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1}_{\llbracket{\Delta}\rrbracket};[\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Delta,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};

\displaystyle[\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{A}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}[\gamma]a:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma]s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash[\gamma]t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma](\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:s,\iota_{r}\;{y}:t\})\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

as desired.

•

Assume
r=s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i}
. Define
\mathsf{R}=(\ell_{i}(A_{i}),)_{i}
and
S=\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket
. We have by induction that,
for all
i
,

(230)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\gamma:\Gamma\mapsto\Delta}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{(\gamma,x_{i}\mapsto x_{i}):(\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i})\mapsto(\Delta,x_{i}:A_{i})}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash[\gamma,x_{i}\mapsto x_{i}]t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash[\gamma]t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket

and therefore that

(231)

\displaystyle S\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\mathsf{lsem}_{\Delta,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i})

\displaystyle=S\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Delta,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket,]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[S\otimes\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Delta,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket,]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash[\gamma]t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket,]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[\gamma]t_{i}\},)_{i})

It follows that

(232)

\displaystyle S;\llbracket{\Delta\vdash s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=S;\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Delta\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket});\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{lsem}_{\Delta,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i})]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(S;\llbracket{\Delta\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket});S\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{lsem}_{\Delta,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i})]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma]s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket});\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},S\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{lsem}_{\Delta,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i})]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma]s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket});\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[\gamma]t_{i}\},)_{i})]

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\gamma]s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[\gamma]t_{i}\},)_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

as desired.

Composition of substitutions then follows by a trivial induction, as does substitution for label
substitutions.
∎

B.4.
Label Substitution

Lemma B.4 (Label Substitution Injection).

For
\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}
and
\mathsf{L}(\ell)=A
, we have

(233)

\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\gamma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

Proof.

We proceed by induction on
\mathsf{L}
:

•

If
\mathsf{L}=\cdot
, then
\mathsf{L}(\ell)=A
is a contradiction

•

If
\mathsf{L}=\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\ell(A)
, then

\sigma=\sigma^{\prime},\ell(x)\mapsto[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)
, so we have

(234)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L},\ell(A)\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\iota_{r};\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma^{\prime}:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

•

If
\mathsf{L}=\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\kappa(A)
, then

\sigma=\sigma^{\prime},\kappa(x)\mapsto[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\kappa\;x)
, so by
induction we have

(235)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L},\ell(A)\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\iota_{\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\ell};\iota_{l});\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma^{\prime}:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,x:B\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\kappa\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\iota_{\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\ell};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma^{\prime}:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma^{\prime}](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

∎

Lemma B.5 (Label Substitution Splitting).

For
\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}
, where
\mathsf{L}=(\ell_{i}(A_{i}),)_{i}
, we have

(236)

\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha_{\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash\sigma_{i}\;x_{i}\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,]_{i}

and therefore

(237)

\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash\sigma_{i}\;x_{i}\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,]_{i}=\alpha_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

In particular, we have that, given
\forall i,\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{K}
,

(238)

\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{A_{i}\},)_{i}):\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha_{\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,]_{i}

Lemma B.6 (Label Substitution Extension).

Given
\sigma=\sigma_{l},\sigma_{r}
,

\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\mathsf{R}^{\prime}
,

\Gamma\vdash\sigma_{l}:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{L}^{\prime}
,

\Gamma\vdash\sigma_{r}:\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{R}^{\prime}
, we have

(239)

\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\mathsf{R}^{\prime}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\llbracket{\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma_{l}:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{L}^{\prime}}\rrbracket+\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma_{r}:\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{R}^{\prime}}\rrbracket;\alpha_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}^{\prime},\mathsf{R}^{\prime}}\rrbracket}

In particular, for
\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}
, we have

(240)

\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash{\sigma}^{\upharpoonleft}:\mathsf{R},\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{R},\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket+\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1};\pi_{r}+\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{R},\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket}

(241)

\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}:\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\llbracket{\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket}

since

(242)

\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{id}:\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{R}=\pi_{r}

\soundnesslsubst

*

Proof.

Fix
\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}
. We begin by proving the soundness of label
substitution for regions as follows: assuming
\Gamma\vdash r\rhd\mathsf{L}
, we proceed by induction
on
r
as follows:

•

If
r=\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;a
, then by Lemma
233
we have that

(243)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;a)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[a/x][\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\bot}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;x)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\bot}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\bot}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\iota_{\mathsf{L},\ell});\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{br}\;\ell\;a\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

as desired.

•

If
r=(\mathsf{let}\;x=a;t)
, then we have by induction that

(244)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{let}\;x=a;t)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{let}\;x=a;[\sigma]t\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma]t\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{let}\;x=a;t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

as desired.

•

If
r=(\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;t)
, then we have by induction that

(245)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;t)\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;[\sigma]t\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash[\sigma]t\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);(\pi_{l};\pi_{l})\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=a;t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

as desired, since

•

If
r=\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:s,\iota_{r}\;{y}:t\}
, then we have by induction that

(246)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\sigma](\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:s,\iota_{r}\;{y}:t\})\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:[\sigma]s,\iota_{r}\;{y}:[\sigma]t\}\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A+B}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash[\sigma]s\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash[\sigma]t\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A+B}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};[

\displaystyle\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket,

\displaystyle\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A+B}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};

\displaystyle[\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket,\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket];\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A+B}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash t\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket]);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\mathsf{case}\;a\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:s,\iota_{r}\;{y}:t\}\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

as desired.

•

If
r=s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i}
, then we have by induction,
taking
\mathsf{R}=(\ell_{i}(A_{i}),)_{i}
,

(247)

\displaystyle\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{K}}([{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]s)=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]s\rhd\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}:\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\llbracket{\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash s\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r}

and

(248)

\displaystyle\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{K}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]t_{i}\},)_{i})=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}:\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket]_{i};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}:\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{j}\llbracket{A_{j}}\rrbracket}]_{i};\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{j}\llbracket{A_{j}}\rrbracket},]_{i});\pi_{l}\otimes-;\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket,]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket});\pi_{l}\otimes-;\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i}));\pi_{l}\otimes-;\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\pi_{r}

Letting
L=\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i})
and

S=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket
, we have that

(249)

\displaystyle\mathsf{rcase}(\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{K}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]t_{i}\},)_{i}))

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(L);\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};S+\pi_{r});\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(L));(\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket)\otimes(\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};S+\pi_{r});\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(L);{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}\otimes-;\alpha;(\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket)\otimes(\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};S+\pi_{r});\pi_{l}\otimes-;\delta^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(L);(\pi_{l};{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket})\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\delta^{-1};S+\pi_{r});\delta^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(L);(\pi_{l};{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket})\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\delta^{-1};\delta^{-1};(\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket)\otimes S+(\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket)\otimes\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(L);\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\delta^{-1};({\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes S)+({\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rcase}(L);{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes S+\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket

implying by naturality that

(250)

\displaystyle\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{K}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]t_{i}\},)_{i}))

\displaystyle=(\mathsf{rcase}(L);{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes S+\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket)^{\dagger};\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger};{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes S;\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger};S

Hence, we have that

(251)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[\sigma](s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i})\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]t_{i}\},)_{i}\rhd\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{K}}([{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]s));\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{K}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonright}]t_{i}\},)_{i}))]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};S+\pi_{r});\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger};S]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s)));\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\delta^{-1};S+\pi_{r});\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger};S]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s)));\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\delta^{-1};\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes S;\pi_{r},\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r};(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger};S]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s)));\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\delta^{-1};\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r};(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger}];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));{\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\delta^{-1};\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r};(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger}];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};[\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\pi_{r},\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r};(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger}];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};[\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r},(\mathsf{rcase}(L))^{\dagger}];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};[\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r},\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{rfix}(L));\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket)];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};[\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r},\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\mathsf{rfix}(L)];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\delta^{-1};\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\pi_{r},\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\mathsf{rfix}(L)];S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\Delta_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}\otimes(\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(L)]);S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s)));\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(L)]);S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(s));\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(L)]);S

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash s\;\mathsf{where}\;(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash\sigma:\mathsf{L}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket

as desired.

Composition of label substitutions then follows by a trivial induction.
∎

B.5.
Equational Theory

\soundnesseqn

*

Proof.

We begin our proof by showing soundness of the equational theory for expressions, i.e.

(a)
, by rule induction.

•

Congruence
: these follow trivially by induction

•

initial
,
terminal
: both of these follow trivially from the universal
property of the initial/terminal object, respectively.

•

let
1
-
\beta
: this follows directly from Corollary
5.11

•

let
1
-
\eta
: we have

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;x:{A}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle={\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}x:{A}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle={\Delta}_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\pi_{r}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket

as desired.

let
1
-op
: we have

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;\mathsf{let}\;y=f\;x;\;c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}f\;x:{B}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\pi_{r};\llbracket{f}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\pi_{r};\llbracket{f}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{f}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;y=f\;a;\;c:{C}}\rrbracket

as desired. The
let
1
-abort
case is analogous.

let
1
-let
1
: we have that

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;y=(\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;b;\;c):{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{B}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{B}}\rrbracket);\pi_{l}\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{B}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;\mathsf{let}\;y=b;\;c:{C}}\rrbracket

as desired.

let
1
-let
2
: we have that

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;z=(\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=e;\;c);\;d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B});\alpha;\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket);(\pi_{l};\pi_{l})\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{C}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B});\alpha;\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket);\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=e;\;\mathsf{let}\;z=c;\;d:{D}}\rrbracket

as desired.

let
1
-case
: follows from the properties of the coproduct; in particular, we have
that

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{case}\;e\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:\mathsf{let}\;z=a;\;d,\iota_{r}\;{y}:\mathsf{let}\;z=b;\;d\}:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1};[

\displaystyle\qquad\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{C}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket,

\displaystyle\qquad\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{C}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1};[

\displaystyle\qquad\Delta\otimes\llbracket{A}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{C}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket,

\displaystyle\qquad\Delta\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{C}}\rrbracket;\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1};[

\displaystyle\qquad\Delta\otimes\llbracket{A}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{C}}\rrbracket,

\displaystyle\qquad\Delta\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{C}}\rrbracket];\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\Delta\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1};

\displaystyle\qquad[\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{C}}\rrbracket,\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{C}}\rrbracket];\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\Delta\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{C}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{C}}\rrbracket]);

\displaystyle\qquad\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A+B}}\rrbracket;\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{C}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}b:{C}}\rrbracket]);

\displaystyle\qquad\llbracket{\Gamma,z:C\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{D}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;z=(\mathsf{case}\;e\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:a,\iota_{r}\;{y}:b\});\;d:{D}}\rrbracket

•

let
2
-bind
: we have

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;z=e;\;\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=z;\;c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma,z:A\otimes B\vdash_{\epsilon}z:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,z:A\otimes B,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\mathsf{let}(\pi_{r});\pi_{l}\otimes(\llbracket{A}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{B}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\pi_{r});\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket);\alpha;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=e;\;c:{C}}\rrbracket

•

let
2
-
\eta
: follows from the properties of the product; in particular,
we have that

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;(x,y)=e;\;(x,y):{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket;\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\bot}x:{A}}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A,y:B\vdash_{\bot}y:{B}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket;\Delta;(\pi_{l};\pi_{r})\otimes\pi_{r}=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}e:{A\otimes B}}\rrbracket

•

case-inl
: follows from the properties of the coproduct and inverse distributor; in
particular, we have that

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{case}\;\iota_{l}\;{a}\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:c,\iota_{r}\;{y}:d\}:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes(\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\iota_{l});\delta^{-1};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{C}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\iota_{l};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket,\llbracket{\Gamma,y:B\vdash_{\epsilon}d:{C}}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=\Delta;\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}a:{A}}\rrbracket;\iota_{l};\llbracket{\Gamma,x:A\vdash_{\epsilon}c:{C}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{let}\;x=a;\;c:{C}}\rrbracket

We can validate
case-inr
analogously

case-
\eta
: follows from the properties of the coproduct and distributor; in

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash_{\epsilon}\mathsf{case}\;e\;\{\iota_{l}\;{x}:\iota_{l}\;{x},\iota_{r}\;{y}:\iota_{r}\;{y}\}:{A+B}}\rrbracket

and

(263)

\displaystyle\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{(\kappa_{j}(x_{j})\mapsto t_{j},)}^{\upharpoonleft}](\sigma_{i}\;x_{i})\},)_{i})

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\mathsf{rlet}(\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash\sigma_{i}\;x_{i}\rhd\mathsf{R}^{\prime}}\rrbracket)]_{i};G;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rlet}(\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash\sigma_{i}\;x_{i}\rhd\mathsf{R}^{\prime}}\rrbracket]_{i});G;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rlet}(\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash\sigma_{i}\;x_{i}\rhd\mathsf{R}^{\prime}}\rrbracket]_{i});G;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rlet}(\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha_{\llbracket{\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket};S);G;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rlet}(S^{\prime});\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha_{\llbracket{\mathsf{R^{\prime}}}\rrbracket};\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash(\kappa_{j}(x_{j})\mapsto t_{j},)_{j}:\mathsf{R}^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rlet}(S^{\prime});\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{R}^{\prime}{\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}}\rrbracket,]_{i};\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{A_{i}}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{rlet}(S^{\prime});L

We therefore have

(264)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonleft}]r\;\mathsf{where}\;(\kappa_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonleft}]t_{i}\},)_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}([{\sigma}^{\upharpoonleft}]r));\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\kappa_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{\sigma}^{\upharpoonleft}]t_{i}\},)_{i}))]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(r));\delta^{-1};\pi_{r}+S^{\prime});\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{rcase}(L);\pi_{r}+S^{\prime})]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(r));\delta^{-1};\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\mathsf{rlet}(S);[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{rcase}(L);\pi_{r}+S^{\prime})]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(r));\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rlet}(S^{\prime});\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{rcase}(L);\pi_{r}+S^{\prime})]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(r));\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{rlet}(S^{\prime});L)]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{let}(\mathsf{esem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(r));\delta^{-1};[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}((\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{[{(\kappa_{j}(x_{j})\mapsto t_{j},)}^{\upharpoonleft}](\sigma_{i}\;x_{i})\},)_{i}))]

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash r\;\mathsf{where}\;[{(\kappa_{j}(x_{j})\mapsto t_{j},)}^{\upharpoonleft}](\sigma_{i}\;x_{i}),)_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

as desired.

All other cases are analogous to those for expressions, and so omitted.
∎

Lemma B.7 (
\mathsf{where}
-fusion).

The rewrite rules
cfg-fuse
1
(Eqn.
16
) and
cfg-fuse
2

(Eqn.
17
) are sound.

Proof.

We begin with
cfg-fuse
1
. Let
\mathsf{R}=(\ell_{j}(A_{j}),)_{j}
,
\mathsf{K}=(\kappa_{k}(B_{k}),)_{k}
,
S=(\kappa_{i}(y_{i}):\{s_{i}\},)_{i}
,

T=(\ell_{i}(x_{i}):\{t_{i}\},)_{j}
, and
D_{S}=\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}}(S)
,
D_{T}=\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(T)
,

D_{G}=\mathsf{lsem}_{\Gamma,\mathsf{L}}(S,T)
We have that

(265)

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Gamma\vdash(r\;\mathsf{where}\;S)\;\mathsf{where}\;T\rhd\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\mathsf{rfix}(D_{G})

\displaystyle=[\mathsf{rfix}(D_{T}),\mathsf{rfix}(D_{S});\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{j}\llbracket{A_{j}}\rrbracket}\gg[\pi_{r},\mathsf{rfix}(D_{T})]_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}]_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}

We note that, by re-association and weakening, we have that

(267)

\displaystyle D_{G}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket\otimes\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{R}}\rrbracket+\llbracket{\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket};\delta^{-1};[\delta^{-1}_{\Sigma};[\llbracket{\Gamma,x_{i}:A_{i}\vdash t_{i}\rhd\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R},\mathsf{K}}\rrbracket;\alpha^{+}_{\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+\Sigma_{j}\llbracket{C_{j}}\rrbracket},]_{i},D_{S}]

\displaystyle=\alpha^{+\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}_{\mathsf{R}+\mathsf{K}}\gg[D_{T}\gg\llbracket{\mathsf{L}}\rrbracket+_{R}(\iota_{l}^{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket};\alpha^{+}_{\Sigma_{i}\llbracket{C_{i}}\rrbracket}),]_{\llbracket{\Gamma}\rrbracket}

where
C_{1..k}=A_{1..k}
and

. We note in particular that

. We can now derive Equation

.

B.6.
Completeness

\completenessexpr

*

Proof.

We begin by showing
\mathsf{Th}^{\otimes}(\Gamma)
is an
\lambda_{\mathsf{SSA}}
expression model by validating
all the equations for a distributive Freyd category. These are formalized as follows:

•

\mathsf{Th}^{\otimes}(\Gamma)
is a category: formalized in

Rewrite/Term/Compose/Seq.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}^{\otimes}(\Gamma)
is a Freyd category: formalized in

Rewrite/Term/Compose/Product.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}^{\otimes}(\Gamma)
has coproducts: formalized in

Rewrite/Term/Compose/Sum.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}^{\otimes}(\Gamma)
is distributive: formalized in

Rewrite/Term/Compose/Distrib.lean

We then prove that the packing of each type constructor is equivalent to its denotational
semantics in
\mathsf{Th}^{\otimes}(\Gamma)
in
Rewrite/Term/Compose/Completeness.lean
.
Finally, we show that packing and unpacking are mutual inverses for
\Gamma
pure in

Term.Eqv.packed_unpacked
and
Term.Eqv.unpacked_packed
in

Rewrite/Term/Structural/Product.lean
, completing the proof of initiality.
∎

\completenessregions

*

Proof.

We begin by showing
\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
is an
\lambda_{\mathsf{SSA}}
region model by validating all
the equations for a distributive Elgot category. These are formalized as follows:

•

\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
is a category: formalized in

Rewrite/Region/Compose/Seq.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
is a Freyd category: formalized in

Rewrite/Region/Compose/Product.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
has coproducts: formalized in

Rewrite/Region/Compose/Sum.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
is distributive: formalized in

Rewrite/Region/Compose/Distrib.lean

•

\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
is a strong Elgot category: formalized in

Rewrite/Region/Compose/Elgot.lean

We then prove that the packing of each type constructor is equivalent to its denotational
semantics in
\mathsf{Th}(\Gamma,\mathsf{L})
in
Rewrite/Region/Compose/Completeness.lean
.
Finally, we show that packing and unpacking are mutual inverses for
\Gamma
pure in

Region.Eqv.packed_unpacked
and
Region.Eqv.unpacked_packed
in

Rewrite/Region/Structural.lean
, completing the proof of initiality.
∎
